. Check that no bridge port completed a transaction after the read (`CHK-J2A-BUS-REQ`).

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-J2A-GEOMETRY` |DTMCS and `sbcs` of every bridge publish the geometry of the bench configuration table
|`CHK-J2A-FAULT-STATUS` |Every DMI capture reports status 0, and `sberror` and `sbbusyerror` read 0 after every access
|`CHK-AXI-COMPLETION` |`sbbusy` clears within eight `sbcs` reads of every access
|`CHK-J2A-BUS-REQ` |Each access completes exactly one single-beat transaction of its direction at its 32-bit address, and each write with its strobes and strobed data; no other transaction reaches a bridge port in the pass or between passes
|`CHK-AXI-WMEM` |After each write, the responder word at its address equals the word written
|`CHK-AXI-RDATA` |The word the read fetches through `sbdata0` equals the first word written, not its neighbour
|`CHK-AXI-RESP`, `CHK-AXI-STRB` |Every response is OKAY, and each write's strobes equal the strobes the sequence armed from the access address and size
|`CHK-AXI-WADDR`, `CHK-AXI-WDATA`, `CHK-AXI-RADDR`, `CHK-AXI-NONVAC` |SV-UVM: the passive AXI environment's address and data comparisons against the stimulus, and at least three responder bursts per pass
|`CHK-AXI-CREDITS`, `CHK-AXI-STREAM-MIN` |cocotb: every armed strobe expectation is consumed, and the bridge stream carries at least two compared transactions
|`jtag2axi_req`, `jtag2axi_status` |Every bridge transaction is the access its DMI operations launched, and every DTMCS and DMI capture equals the bridge model on the bits it predicts
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every pass reads the bench geometry from all three bridges, and on the SEP OTP bridge both writes land once at their addresses and the read-back returns the first word, with every DMI capture at status 0 and zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-24-dtp_jtag2axi_dmi_status_test]]
===== 2.4 `dtp_jtag2axi_dmi_status_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: DTMCS and the sticky DMI status on every bridge

*Test Coverage*:

* All three bridges in a seeded order, each through its DTMCS and DMI TDRs and its manager port, with the seven cases below in a seeded order on each
* DTMCS `dmistat` at 0 and 3, `dmireset` at status 0 and 3, and `dmihardreset`
* DMI ops 0 to 3 at status 0, and each op at status 3
* Status 3 from each of its causes: a capture during a read, an update while a write is in progress, and a Debug Module busy answer
* A read the Debug Module refuses, and a read left in progress while TCK is stopped in Run-Test/Idle

*Test Procedure*:

The cases use raw DMI scans, which clock no Run-Test/Idle cycle after their update. A chained
scan goes from Update-DR straight to Select-DR-Scan, so the next scan captures 2 TCK cycles after
the update, a scan from Run-Test/Idle captures 3 TCK cycles after it, and a DR scan that shifts no
bit updates 4 TCK cycles after it and loads the value it captured. All three fall inside the 6 TCK
cycles in which the operation is in progress. Every case ends
with 64 TCK cycles in Run-Test/Idle and the status at 0.

[arabic]
. Run the geometry gate as in 2.1, clear every debug disable, and reset the TAP.
. For each bridge in a seeded order, set `dmcontrol.dmactive`, write `sbcs` with `sbaccess` at the bridge's data width, and run the seven cases in a seeded order:
a. DTMCS: a `dmireset` scan captures `dmistat` 0. A write of `dmcontrol` chained into a scan that shifts no bit leaves status 3. A `dmihardreset` scan captures `dmistat` 3, a `dmireset` scan captures 3 and clears it, and the next DTMCS scan captures 0. A no-operation scan then captures the address and data of the `dmcontrol` write.
b. Operations: with status 0, a no-operation scan and an op 3 scan each load their seeded address and data, which the next scan captures. A write of a seeded `sbaddress0` value, a read of `sbaddress0`, and a no-operation scan return it.
c. Capture during a read: after a write of a seeded `sbaddress0` value, a read of `sbaddress0` chained into a write scan captures status 3 with the read's own address and data, and the write is ignored. After Run-Test/Idle, an op 3 scan captures status 3 with the `sbaddress0` value and clears it, a no-operation scan captures status 0 with the same value, and a read of `sbaddress0` shows it unchanged.
d. Update during a write: a write of a seeded `sbaddress0` value chained into a scan that shifts no bit updates a no-operation while the write is in progress, which sets status 3. A later `sbdata0` write captures status 3 and is ignored with no bus access. A `dmireset` scan clears the status, and a read of `sbaddress0` returns the value of the first write.
e. Update during a read: after a write of a seeded `sbaddress0` value, a read of `sbaddress0` chained into a scan that shifts no bit captures status 3, so that scan updates op 3, which clears it. The read completes, and a no-operation scan captures status 0 with the `sbaddress0` value.
f. Debug Module busy: set up a System Bus Access write of a seeded word at a seeded address, hold the bridge's write response, and write `sbdata0`, which starts the write. A write of `sbaddress0` is refused: the op 3 scan after it captures status 3 with the refused address and data and clears it, and `sbcs` reads `sbbusy` and `sbbusyerror` set. Release the hold, check the completed write (`CHK-J2A-BUS-REQ`, `CHK-AXI-WMEM`), and poll `sbcs` until `sbbusy` clears with `sbbusyerror` still set. A read of `sbdata0` is then refused: the no-operation and op 3 scans after it capture status 3 and `0xB051B051`. Write `sbcs` with `sbbusyerror` set, which clears it, and poll `sbcs` until `sbbusy` clears with `sberror` and `sbbusyerror` at 0.
g. TCK stopped: after a write of a seeded `sbaddress0` value, a read of `sbaddress0` ends in Run-Test/Idle, where TCK stops for 200 to 1000 seeded system clock cycles. The next scan, a read of `dmstatus`, captures status 3 with the first read's address and data and is ignored. After Run-Test/Idle, an op 3 scan captures status 3 with the `sbaddress0` address and value and clears it, and a no-operation scan captures status 0.
. In SV-UVM, check that each bridge's responder completed at least one burst in the pass (`CHK-AXI-NONVAC`).
. Check that no bridge port completed a transaction other than the held write of each bridge (`CHK-J2A-BUS-REQ`).

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-J2A-GEOMETRY` |DTMCS and `sbcs` of every bridge publish the geometry of the bench configuration table
|`CHK-J2A-FAULT-STATUS` |Every DMI capture and every `dmistat` reports the status its case expects; with the write response held `sbcs` reads `sbbusy` and `sbbusyerror` set, and every other `sbcs` poll reads `sberror` 0 and `sbbusyerror` as its case expects
|`CHK-J2A-DTM-CAPTURE` |Every DTMCS capture reads the bench DTMCS value outside `dmistat`, and every DMI capture a case names carries the address and data the case expects
|`CHK-AXI-COMPLETION` |`sbbusy` clears within eight `sbcs` reads of every poll
|`CHK-J2A-BUS-REQ` |Each held write completes exactly one single-beat transaction on its bridge's port with its address, size, strobes, and strobed data; no other transaction reaches a bridge port, so the ignored and refused accesses launch none
|`CHK-AXI-WMEM` |After the release, the responder word at the held write's address equals the word written
|`CHK-AXI-RESP`, `CHK-AXI-STRB` |Every response is OKAY, and each write's strobes equal the strobes the sequence armed from the access address and size
|`CHK-AXI-WADDR`, `CHK-AXI-WDATA`, `CHK-AXI-NONVAC` |SV-UVM: the passive AXI environment's address and data comparisons against the stimulus, and at least one responder burst per bridge per pass
|`CHK-AXI-CREDITS`, `CHK-AXI-STREAM-MIN` |cocotb: every armed strobe expectation is consumed, and each bridge stream carries at least one compared transaction
|`jtag2axi_req`, `jtag2axi_status` |Every bridge transaction is the access its DMI operations launched, and every DTMCS and DMI capture, busy ones included, equals the bridge model on the bits it predicts
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every pass reads the bench geometry from all three bridges, and on each bridge every capture of the seven cases carries the status, address, and data its case expects, only the held write reaches the bridge port, and the scoreboard and reference models report zero errors.

'''''

[[sec-25-dtp_jtag2axi_dm_regs_test]]
===== 2.5 `dtp_jtag2axi_dm_regs_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: Debug Module activation and the `sbcs` geometry on every bridge

*Test Coverage*:

* All three bridges in a seeded order, each through its DMI TDR and its manager port
* `dmcontrol`, `sbcs`, `sbaddress0`, and `sbdata0` read and written with `dmactive` 0 and 1, and `sbaddress1` and `sbdata1` on the SMC fabric bridge
* Each access that starts a bus access while `dmactive` is 1, made while it is 0
* An `sbcs` write that sets every read-only field to the inverse of the bridge's geometry

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Run the geometry gate as in 2.1, clear every debug disable, and reset the TAP.
. For each bridge in a seeded order:
a. Deactivate: write `dmcontrol` with `ndmreset` and `dmactive` set, `sbcs` with `sbaccess` one above the bridge's data width and `sbautoincrement` and `sbreadondata` set, then seeded nonzero values to `sbaddress1` and `sbdata1` where the bridge has them, to `sbaddress0`, aligned to that size, and to `sbdata0`, whose write sets `sberror` 4 with no bus access. `sbcs` reads `sberror` 4 with the written fields, and each register written reads its value. Write `dmcontrol` with 0: `dmcontrol` then reads 0, `sbcs` reads `0x00040000`, and each `sbaddress` and `sbdata` register reads 0.
b. Inactive: write `dmcontrol` with `ndmreset` set, seeded fields above it, and `dmactive` 0, `sbcs` with `sbreadonaddr`, `sbautoincrement`, and `sbreadondata` set and a seeded `sbaccess` up to the data width, and seeded `sbaddress1` and `sbaddress0` values aligned to that size. `dmcontrol`, `sbcs`, each `sbaddress` register, and `sbdata0` read their reset values. Write seeded `sbdata1` and `sbdata0` values, after which `sbcs` and each `sbdata` register read their reset values. No transaction reaches the port.
c. Reactivate: write `dmcontrol` with 1. `dmcontrol` reads 1, `sbcs` reads the geometry with `sbaccess` 2 and the other writable fields 0, and each `sbaddress` and `sbdata` register reads 0. Write `sbcs` with `sbversion`, `sbbusy`, `sbasize`, and `sbaccess128` to `sbaccess8` the inverse of the geometry, `sbbusyerror`, `sberror`, and bits 28:23 at 0, and seeded writable fields other than their reset values; `sbcs` reads the geometry with the written fields. Write a seeded word and its neighbour, and read the first back, as in 2.1.
. In SV-UVM, check that each bridge's responder completed at least one burst in the pass (`CHK-AXI-NONVAC`).
. Check that no bridge port completed a transaction other than the writes and the read of step c (`CHK-J2A-BUS-REQ`).

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-J2A-GEOMETRY` |DTMCS and `sbcs` of every bridge publish the geometry of the bench configuration table, and `sbcs` keeps it after the write of step c
|`CHK-J2A-DM-ACTIVE` |Every read of `dmcontrol`, `sbcs`, `sbaddress`, and `sbdata` in steps a to c returns the value its step expects
|`CHK-J2A-BUS-REQ` |No transaction reaches a bridge port while `dmactive` is 0, and each write and the read of step c completes exactly one single-beat transaction with its address, size, strobes, and strobed data
|`CHK-J2A-FAULT-STATUS` |Every DMI capture reports status 0, and every `sbcs` poll reads `sberror` and `sbbusyerror` 0
|`CHK-AXI-COMPLETION` |`sbbusy` clears within eight `sbcs` reads of every poll
|`CHK-AXI-WMEM`, `CHK-AXI-RDATA` |The responder word at each write's address equals the word written, and the read returns the first word
|`CHK-AXI-RESP`, `CHK-AXI-STRB` |Every response is OKAY, and each write's strobes equal the strobes the sequence armed from the access address and size
|`CHK-AXI-WADDR`, `CHK-AXI-WDATA`, `CHK-AXI-RADDR`, `CHK-AXI-NONVAC` |SV-UVM: the passive AXI environment's address and data comparisons against the stimulus, and at least one responder burst per bridge per pass
|`CHK-AXI-CREDITS`, `CHK-AXI-STREAM-MIN` |cocotb: every armed strobe expectation is consumed, and each bridge stream carries at least one compared transaction
|`jtag2axi_req`, `jtag2axi_status` |Every bridge transaction is the access its DMI operations launched, and every DMI capture equals the bridge model on the bits it predicts
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every pass reads the bench geometry from all three bridges, and on each bridge every read around a change of `dmactive` returns the value its step expects, no transaction reaches the port while `dmactive` is 0, and the scoreboard and reference models report zero errors.

'''''

[[sec-26-dtp_jtag2axi_sba_sizes_test]]
===== 2.6 `dtp_jtag2axi_sba_sizes_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: System Bus Access sizes, byte lanes, and autoincrement on every bridge

*Test Coverage*:

* All three bridges in a seeded order, each through its DMI TDR and its manager port
* Every `sbaccess` size up to the bridge's data width at every offset in the beat aligned to it: 1, 2, 4, and 8 bytes on the SMC fabric bridge, and 1, 2, and 4 on the two OTP bridges, each read and written
* An `sbautoincrement` stream of writes at each size, which crosses address bit 32 on the SMC fabric bridge, and an autoincrementing read at each size

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Run the geometry gate as in 2.1, clear every debug disable, and reset the TAP.
. For each bridge in a seeded order:
a. Lanes: write a full-width word of distinct seeded bytes at a seeded beat. Read each size at each aligned offset in a seeded order and compare the bytes of the access with the bytes of the word at that offset. Then write a seeded value, different from the bytes it replaces, at each size and aligned offset in a seeded order, and after each write compare the whole beat in the responder memory with the beat the writes so far give.
b. Autoincrement, at each size up to the data width in a seeded order: draw a stream that writes two or three whole beats, so every byte a read of the stream returns is one the stream wrote. On the SMC fabric bridge the stream starts below a multiple of 2^32 under seeded address bits and ends above it; on the OTP bridges it starts at a seeded beat. Write `sbcs` with `sbautoincrement` and the size, then `sbaddress1` where the bridge has it and `sbaddress0` once, then for each access `sbdata1` for an 8-byte access and a distinct seeded `sbdata0` value. Each access reaches the port at the start address plus its index times the size, `sbaddress` then reads the start address plus the stream's length times the size, and the responder memory holds each value. Then write `sbcs` with `sbreadonaddr` and `sbautoincrement` and `sbaddress0` with one element's address, on the SMC fabric bridge the last element below the multiple of 2^32. The read returns that element's value, and `sbaddress` reads its address plus the size.
. In SV-UVM, check that each bridge's responder completed at least one burst in the pass (`CHK-AXI-NONVAC`).
. Check that no bridge port completed a transaction other than the accesses of steps a and b (`CHK-J2A-BUS-REQ`).

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-J2A-GEOMETRY` |DTMCS and `sbcs` of every bridge publish the geometry of the bench configuration table
|`CHK-J2A-AUTOINC` |`sbaddress` reads the address the autoincrement stream and the autoincrementing read of step b advance it to
|`CHK-J2A-BUS-REQ` |Each access completes exactly one single-beat transaction at the address the sequence computes, at its size on the AXI4 bridge, and for a write with its strobes and strobed data
|`CHK-J2A-FAULT-STATUS` |Every DMI capture reports status 0, and every `sbcs` poll reads `sberror` and `sbbusyerror` 0
|`CHK-AXI-COMPLETION` |`sbbusy` clears within eight `sbcs` reads of every poll
|`CHK-AXI-WMEM`, `CHK-AXI-RDATA` |The responder beat after each write of step a, and each value of the stream, equal what the writes give, and each read returns the bytes of its access
|`CHK-AXI-RESP`, `CHK-AXI-STRB` |Every response is OKAY, and each write's strobes equal the strobes the sequence armed from the access address and size
|`CHK-AXI-WADDR`, `CHK-AXI-WDATA`, `CHK-AXI-RADDR`, `CHK-AXI-NONVAC` |SV-UVM: the passive AXI environment's address and data comparisons against the stimulus, and at least one responder burst per bridge per pass
|`CHK-AXI-CREDITS`, `CHK-AXI-STREAM-MIN` |cocotb: every armed strobe expectation is consumed, and each bridge stream carries at least one compared transaction
|`jtag2axi_req`, `jtag2axi_status` |Every bridge transaction is the access its DMI operations launched, and every DMI capture equals the bridge model on the bits it predicts
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every pass reads the bench geometry from all three bridges, and on each bridge every access of every size and aligned offset carries exactly its bytes, the autoincremented addresses advance by the access size, and the scoreboard and reference models report zero errors.

'''''

[[sec-27-dtp_jtag2axi_sba_errors_test]]
===== 2.7 `dtp_jtag2axi_sba_errors_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: System Bus Access bus and size errors, the accesses an error refuses, its clear, and recovery on every bridge

*Test Coverage*:

* All three bridges in a seeded order, each through its DMI TDR and its manager port
* SLVERR and DECERR from the bridge's responder, each answering a write and a read of a seeded size at a seeded aligned offset
* Every `sbaccess` size above the bridge's data width, 4 to 7 on the SMC fabric bridge and 3 to 7 on the two OTP bridges, each started by an `sbdata0` write and by an `sbaddress0` write
* An `sbdata0` write and an `sbaddress0` write with `sbreadonaddr` while `sberror` is 2, and `sbcs` writes that keep and clear it
* An error held on each bridge while the bridges after it run

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Run the geometry gate as in 2.1, clear every debug disable, and reset the TAP.
. For each bridge in a seeded order:
a. Size errors, at each size above the data width and each trigger in a seeded order: write `sbcs` with the size, and for a read `sbreadonaddr`, then `sbaddress0` with a seeded address aligned to the size, and for a write a seeded `sbdata0`. `sbcs` reads `sberror` 4, and no transaction reaches the port. Clear: write `sbcs` with a seeded nonzero `sberror` field, after which `sbcs` reads `sberror` 0.
b. Bus errors, SLVERR and DECERR on a write and on a read in a seeded order: fill a seeded beat with a seeded word, arm the responder to answer the next access of the direction at that beat with the error, and arm the AXI checks to expect it. Read, or write data that differs from the beat in the bytes of the access, at a seeded size and aligned offset. `sbcs` reads `sberror` 2 and the beat keeps the fill word. Then, in a seeded order, set up a write with `sbcs` and `sbaddress0` and write `sbdata0`, and set up a read with `sbcs` and `sbreadonaddr` and write `sbaddress0`: no transaction reaches the port, and `sbcs` reads `sberror` 2 after each. The beat still holds the fill word. Clear as in step a, then write a seeded word at the beat and another at the next beat and read the beat back, as in 2.1.
c. Held error: make one more bus error of a seeded kind as in step b, and leave `sberror` at 2.
. In SV-UVM, check that each bridge's responder completed at least one burst in the pass (`CHK-AXI-NONVAC`).
. For each bridge in the same order, check that `sbcs` still reads `sberror` 2, clear it as in step a, and write, write the neighbour, and read back at a seeded beat as in 2.1.
. Check that no bridge port completed a transaction other than the fills, the errored accesses, and the recovery accesses (`CHK-J2A-BUS-REQ`).

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-J2A-SBA-ERROR` |`sbcs` reads `sberror` 4 after each size error and 2 after each bus error, 2 after each refused access, 0 after each clear, and 2 on each bridge once the bridges after it have run
|`CHK-J2A-GEOMETRY` |DTMCS and `sbcs` of every bridge publish the geometry of the bench configuration table
|`CHK-J2A-BUS-REQ` |Each fill, errored access, and recovery access completes exactly one single-beat transaction at the address the sequence computes, at its size on the AXI4 bridge, and for a write with its strobes and strobed data; a size error and a refused access complete none
|`CHK-J2A-FAULT-STATUS` |Every DMI capture reports status 0, and the `sbcs` poll after each access reads the `sberror` the access ends with and `sbbusyerror` 0
|`CHK-AXI-COMPLETION` |`sbbusy` clears within eight `sbcs` reads of every poll
|`CHK-AXI-WMEM`, `CHK-AXI-RDATA` |The beat keeps its fill word after the errored access and after the refused accesses, each recovery write lands, and each recovery read returns the word written
|`CHK-AXI-RESP`, `CHK-AXI-STRB` |Every response other than an injected one is OKAY, and each write's strobes equal the strobes the sequence armed from the access address and size
|`CHK-AXI-RESP-EXPECTED`, `CHK-AXI-CREDITS`, `CHK-AXI-STREAM-MIN` |cocotb: each injected response consumes the expectation the scenario armed for it, every armed expectation is consumed, and each bridge stream carries at least one compared transaction
|`CHK-AXI-ERR-INJ`, `CHK-AXI-WADDR`, `CHK-AXI-WDATA`, `CHK-AXI-RADDR`, `CHK-AXI-NONVAC` |SV-UVM: each injected response is one the scenario armed and none is left armed at the end, the passive AXI environment's address and data comparisons against the stimulus, and at least one responder burst per bridge per pass
|`jtag2axi_req`, `jtag2axi_status` |Every bridge transaction is the access its DMI operations launched, and every DMI capture equals the bridge model on the bits it predicts, `sberror` among them
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every pass reads the bench geometry from all three bridges, and on each bridge every injected response and oversized access sets the `sberror` its kind gives, no access starts while it is set, each clear returns it to 0 and the next access completes, each bridge keeps its own error while the others run, and the scoreboard and reference models report zero errors.

'''''

[[sec-28-dtp_jtag2axi_backpressure_test]]
===== 2.8 `dtp_jtag2axi_backpressure_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: Transfer attributes, one transfer at a time, and System Bus Access under seeded READY stalls and response delays on every bridge

*Test Coverage*:

* All three bridges in a seeded order, each through its DMI TDR and its manager port
* AWREADY, WREADY, and ARREADY stalls and B and R delays, each channel from its own seeded stream in one of four modes: none, 1 to 8 cycles, one to two `sbcs` polls in system clock cycles, and a seeded mix of up to 8 cycles and 10 to 40 percent long draws
* Every mode on every channel, each channel other than W in its own seeded order, so a long stall on one channel meets a short or no stall on another
* W in the mode paired with AW's, none with none, short with long, long with short, and the mix with the mix, so W is accepted before, with, and after its AW
* Full-beat writes and reads, and writes and reads of a seeded size at a seeded aligned offset
* A second access requested while one is open: an `sbdata0` write under a held write and an `sbaddress0` write under a held read

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Run the geometry gate as in 2.1, clear every debug disable, and reset the TAP.
. For each bridge in a seeded order:
a. Measure one `sbcs` poll, a read and the scan that fetches it, in system clock cycles, and read the port's open-request counter.
b. Draw a seeded order of the four modes for AW, AR, B, and R, and give W the mode paired with AW's at each step. For each of the four steps, give each channel a new seeded stream in its mode for that step; in SV-UVM the responder also accepts a write's W beat alongside its AW, so the two stalls decide which handshake lands first.
c. Under each step, twice: write a seeded word at a seeded beat and another at the next beat, read the beat back as in 2.1, then write seeded bytes of a seeded size at a seeded aligned offset of the beat and read them back.
d. Return the responder to READY high and immediate responses.
e. Hold the write response and start a full-beat write of a seeded word at a seeded beat, then write a seeded word to `sbdata0`, which would start the next write. The scan after it captures status busy and clears it, `sbcs` reads `sbbusy` and `sbbusyerror` 1, and the open-request counter has not advanced since the second write (`CHK-J2A-ONE-XFER`). Release the hold, check the write's bus request and that the beat holds its own word, and clear `sbbusyerror`. Then do the same with the read response held, a read of that beat started by `sbaddress0`, and a second `sbaddress0` write, and check that the read returns the word.
f. Check that the open-request counter has not advanced over the bridge's steps (`CHK-J2A-ONE-XFER`).
. In SV-UVM, check that each bridge's responder completed at least one burst in the pass (`CHK-AXI-NONVAC`).

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-J2A-AXI-ATTR` |Every request carries AxPROT 0, and on the SMC fabric bridge AxLEN 0, INCR, AxCACHE `4'b0010`, and AxID, AxUSER, and WUSER 0
|`CHK-J2A-ONE-XFER` |The port raised no AWVALID or ARVALID in a cycle in which an accepted request waited for its B or last R, counted by `tb_top` on VALID, so a second request shows whether or not the responder accepts it
|`CHK-J2A-GEOMETRY` |DTMCS and `sbcs` of every bridge publish the geometry of the bench configuration table
|`CHK-J2A-BUS-REQ` |Each access completes exactly one single-beat transaction at the address the sequence computes, at its size on the AXI4 bridge, and for a write with its strobes and strobed data
|`CHK-J2A-FAULT-STATUS` |Every DMI capture reports status 0 other than the busy captures of step e, every `sbcs` poll reads `sberror` 0, and `sbbusyerror` is 1 from each refused write of step e until it is cleared and 0 otherwise
|`CHK-AXI-COMPLETION` |`sbbusy` clears within eight `sbcs` reads of every poll
|`CHK-AXI-WMEM`, `CHK-AXI-RDATA` |Each write lands with only its bytes changed, and each read returns the bytes written
|`CHK-AXI-RESP`, `CHK-AXI-STRB` |Every response is OKAY, and each write's strobes equal the strobes the sequence armed from the access address and size
|`CHK-AXI-CREDITS`, `CHK-AXI-STREAM-MIN` |cocotb: every armed expectation is consumed, and each bridge stream carries at least one compared transaction
|`CHK-AXI-WADDR`, `CHK-AXI-WDATA`, `CHK-AXI-RADDR`, `CHK-AXI-NONVAC` |SV-UVM: the passive AXI environment's address and data comparisons against the stimulus, and at least one responder burst per bridge per pass
|`ocah_axi_sva` |On each bridge port, every VALID and its payload hold while READY is low
|`jtag2axi_req`, `jtag2axi_status` |Every bridge transaction is the access its DMI operations launched, and every DMI capture equals the bridge model on the bits it predicts, every busy `sbcs` poll included
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every pass reads the bench geometry from all three bridges, and on each bridge every request carries the specified attributes, no request starts while one is open, an access requested while one is open is refused, every access completes under every stall and delay mode with the data and status it has without them, and the scoreboard and reference models report zero errors.

'''''

[[sec-3-stap-3dcr-and-ijtag-scenarios]]
==== 3. STAP, 3DCR, and iJTAG Scenarios

[[sec-31-dtp_ijtag_sib_all_off_test]]
===== 3.1 `dtp_ijtag_sib_all_off_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, OCAH-local iJTAG SIB model

*Objective*: All SIBs OFF

*Test Coverage*:

* SELECT_IJTAG (IR=0x1A) with every SIB control bit driven to 0 yields the minimum-length scan chain
* DR chain length equals the SIB-control bits only (3 bits for sec_dft, dft, dfd) with none of the bench's instrument stubs (4, 5, 6 bits behind sec_dft, dft, dfd) inserted
* No SIB's host `select` (in `jtag_dfd_host_scan_ctrl_o`, `jtag_dft_host_scan_ctrl_o`, `jtag_dft_secure_host_scan_ctrl_o`) asserts during the scan, and a gated SIB keeps all four of its controls quiet
* Closed SIBs bypass their instrument stubs so no stub capture/update occurs, whatever disable mask is in force (the stubs are the bench's 4-, 5-, and 6-bit registers behind the SIBs; see Verification Scope)

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Load SELECT_IJTAG (IR=0x1A) into the PTAP via `OcahJtagMasterDriver` and program a seeded non-zero SIB pattern, so the all-off result is a transition from open SIBs.
. Shift a composed SIB pattern with all three SIB bits = 0 and pulse Update-DR; check the captured bits against the SIB model.
. Measure the active DR chain length.
a. Shift a seeded marker word ahead of the chain image and locate its MSB in the TDO stream.
b. Verify the latency equals the 3 SIB-control bits only (no instrument register widths added).
. Under a temporal window over the whole scan, verify no SIB select pulses (every stub stays out of the chain); the window also records the DUT's Shift-DR cycles, so a TAP that never shifted cannot satisfy the quiet checks.
. Repeat the all-off programming under a seeded per-SIB disable mask and verify the same outcome.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`sib_all_off_len_chk` |`CHK-SCAN-LEN`: the marker latency through the SELECT_IJTAG chain equals the 3 SIB bits with every instrument stub bypassed
|`sib_all_off_ctrl_chk` |`CHK-SCAN-WIN`: no DFD/DFT/secure-DFT `select` pulses during the observe scan, and a gated SIB's `shift_en`/`capture_en`/`update_en` stay quiet
|`sib_all_off_data_chk` |`CHK-SCAN-CHAIN`: the captured SIB states (all 0) and the marker passthrough match the SIB reference model on every scan, including the transition from the seeded open set
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Chain length collapses to the SIB-control bits, every SIB's host `select` stays low, and observed data matches the SIB reference model, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-32-dtp_ijtag_sib_all_on_test]]
===== 3.2 `dtp_ijtag_sib_all_on_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, OCAH-local iJTAG SIB model

*Objective*: All SIBs ON

*Test Coverage*:

* Before the first scan, with every disable clear, `jtag_dft_secure_host_scan_out_o`, `jtag_dft_host_scan_out_o`, `jtag_dfd_host_scan_out_o`, and `jtag_stap_host_scan_out_o` drive a resolved 0 (the SIB and 3DCR scan stages reset)
* SELECT_IJTAG (IR=0x1A) with all SIB bits = 1 produces the full-length iJTAG scan chain
* DR chain length equals sum of SIB bits plus all three instrument register widths (3 SIBs {plus} 3 instruments)
* All three scan controls (`jtag_dfd_host_scan_ctrl_o`, `jtag_dft_host_scan_ctrl_o`, `jtag_dft_secure_host_scan_ctrl_o`) assert during Shift-DR
* Asserting each SIB's disable alone (`dbg_disable_i.dft_secure`, `dbg_disable_i.dft_nonsecure`, or `dbg_disable_i.dfd`) and all three together, in a seeded order, forces the gated SIBs closed even when the scan pattern requests all-ON, removing their instruments from the chain
* Access is restored when the disables clear

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. After Test-Logic-Reset with every disable clear, and before any IR or DR scan, sample the three iJTAG host scan outputs and the extended STAP host scan output and verify each is a resolved 0.
. Load SELECT_IJTAG (IR=0x1A) via `OcahJtagMasterDriver`.
. Shift a pattern enabling all SIBs (all bits = 1); Update-DR.
. Measure DR chain length by shifting a known marker and counting the delay.
a. Verify length equals the sum of SIB bits plus all three instrument register widths.
. Verify all three host scan-control outputs assert during Shift-DR and instrument data is captured correctly per the SIB model.
. Security gating, for each of four gate vectors in a seeded order: `dbg_disable_i.dft_secure` alone, `dbg_disable_i.dft_nonsecure` alone, `dbg_disable_i.dfd` alone, and all three:
a. Drive the vector's disables with every other disable clear.
b. Re-shift the all-ON pattern; Update-DR.
c. Verify the chain loses exactly the gated SIBs' instruments (SIB bits only when all three are gated) and none of a gated SIB's scan controls pulse across the whole gated window.
. Clear the disables; re-shift all-ON and confirm full chain and all scan controls restored.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`sib_all_on_reset_chk` |`CHK-SCAN-RESET`: every iJTAG host scan output and the extended STAP host scan output is a resolved 0 before the first scan
|`sib_all_on_len_chk` |`CHK-SCAN-LEN`: full chain length matches sum of SIB bits plus all three instrument widths
|`sib_all_on_ctrl_chk` |`CHK-SCAN-WIN`: all three host scan-control outputs assert during Shift-DR
|`sib_all_on_gate_chk` |`CHK-SCAN-WIN`, `CHK-SCAN-LEN`, and `CHK-SCAN-CHAIN`: each gate vector forces exactly its gated SIBs closed and keeps their controls quiet; the chain loses their instruments despite the all-ON request
|`sib_all_on_restore_chk` |`CHK-SCAN-LEN` and `CHK-SCAN-WIN`: full chain and scan controls restored after the disables clear
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The all-ON chain reaches full length with every scan control active, collapses to SIB bits when the SIB disables are asserted, and recovers when they clear, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-33-dtp_ijtag_sib_random_test]]
===== 3.3 `dtp_ijtag_sib_random_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, OCAH-local iJTAG SIB model

*Objective*: Exhaustive/random SIB patterns

*Test Coverage*:

* Every combination of closed, open, and gated SIBs walked exhaustively, the 8 on/off patterns (sec_dft x dft x dfd) with every disable clear among them, closing `cg_ijtag_sib` `cp_pattern` and `x_gating` in every pass
* Predicted chain length matches sum of (SIB bit {plus} instrument stub width when SIB ON and ungated, else SIB bit only); the stubs are 4, 5, 6 bits wide behind sec_dft, dft, dfd, so every open subset has a distinct length
* Only open, ungated instruments see their host `select`, and a gated SIB keeps all four of its controls quiet
* Randomized per-SIB disables (`dft_secure`, `dft_nonsecure`, `dfd`) force gated SIBs closed regardless of scan pattern
* Capture/update data tracks the SIB reference model across mixed configurations: a SIB captures its effective state, an open instrument stub captures the value the previous scan latched into it

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Load SELECT_IJTAG (IR=0x1A) via `OcahJtagMasterDriver`.
. Walk every combination of closed, open, and gated SIBs: for each disable mask of `dft_secure`, `dft_nonsecure`, and `dfd` in a seeded order, every open set of the mask's ungated SIBs, with a seeded request bit for each gated SIB (27 combinations):
a. Shift each (sec_dft, dft, dfd) pattern composed over the current chain, with seeded values for the instruments already open; Update-DR.
b. Shift a seeded marker word ahead of the chain image, locate its MSB in the TDO stream, and verify the latency equals SIB bits plus the widths of the open instruments.
c. Under a temporal window over that scan, verify `select` pulses only for the open instruments and a gated SIB's controls never pulse.
. For 16 additional randomized iterations:
a. Drive a random per-SIB disable mask and a random SIB pattern; Update-DR.
b. Verify gated SIBs (those with their `dbg_disable_i` field asserted) stay closed despite the requested pattern.
c. Verify chain length accounts for forced-closed SIBs and only non-gated enabled instruments see their `select`.
. Compare the captured bits of every scan against the OCAH-local SIB model for each configuration.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`sib_random_len_chk` |`CHK-SCAN-LEN`: the marker latency through the SELECT_IJTAG chain matches the predicted SIB-bit {plus} ungated-instrument-width sum
|`sib_random_ctrl_chk` |`CHK-SCAN-WIN`: `select` pulses only for enabled, ungated instruments; a closed SIB's `select` never pulses
|`sib_random_gate_chk` |`CHK-SCAN-WIN` and `CHK-SCAN-CHAIN`: a gated SIB's `select`/`shift_en`/`capture_en`/`update_en` never pulse and it captures closed under random disable masks
|`sib_random_data_chk` |`CHK-SCAN-CHAIN`: the captured SIB states and instrument registers, and the marker passthrough, match the SIB reference model on every scan
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every closed, open, and gated SIB combination plus the randomized security combinations yield correct chain lengths, scan-control assertions, and gating behavior matching the reference model, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-34-dtp_ijtag_dft_test]]
===== 3.4 `dtp_ijtag_dft_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, OCAH-local iJTAG SIB model

*Objective*: DFT secure/non-secure access

*Test Coverage*:

* Independent access to the secure and non-secure DFT instruments via their SIBs (SELECT_IJTAG, IR=0x1A)
* Both DFT SIBs may be open simultaneously (no hardware mutual exclusion); chain length sums both instruments
* `dbg_disable_i.dft_nonsecure = 1` gates the non-secure DFT SIB; `dbg_disable_i.dft_secure = 1` gates the secure DFT SIB
* Gating one DFT SIB leaves the other accessible (cross-resource isolation), a gated Update-DR does not modify stored SIB state, and access resumes without TAP reset once the disable clears

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Load SELECT_IJTAG (IR=0x1A); enable the non-secure DFT SIB; Update-DR.
a. Verify `jtag_dft_host_scan_ctrl_o` asserts and shift a test pattern through the non-secure chain.
. Disable non-secure, enable secure DFT SIB; Update-DR.
a. Verify `jtag_dft_secure_host_scan_ctrl_o` asserts and shift a test pattern through the secure chain.
. Enable both DFT SIBs; Update-DR; verify both scan controls assert and chain length equals the sum of both instruments.
. Secure gating: assert `dbg_disable_i.dft_secure=1`; verify the secure DFT SIB forced closed (its scan controls never pulse across the gated window) while the non-secure SIB remains openable; clear.
. Non-secure gating: assert `dbg_disable_i.dft_nonsecure=1`; verify the non-secure DFT SIB forced closed while the secure SIB remains openable; clear.
. Stored-state evidence: attempt to open a gated SIB, release the disable without reset, verify the gated open attempt did not stick, then confirm a sanctioned open succeeds.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`dft_access_chk` |`CHK-SCAN-WIN` and `CHK-SCAN-CHAIN`: secure and non-secure DFT chains each accessible via their SIB with correct scan controls
|`dft_parallel_chk` |`CHK-SCAN-LEN`: both DFT SIBs open simultaneously; chain length sums both instruments
|`dft_nonsecure_gate_chk` |`CHK-SCAN-WIN` and `CHK-SCAN-CHAIN`: non-secure DFT SIB forced closed under `dbg_disable_i.dft_nonsecure`
|`dft_secure_gate_chk` |`CHK-SCAN-WIN` and `CHK-SCAN-CHAIN`: secure DFT SIB forced closed under `dbg_disable_i.dft_secure`
|`dft_isolation_chk` |`CHK-SCAN-WIN` and `CHK-SCAN-LEN`: gating one DFT SIB leaves the other accessible
|`dft_gate_restore_chk` |`CHK-SCAN-WIN` and `CHK-SCAN-LEN`: both DFT SIBs restored, without TAP reset, after their disables clear
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Secure and non-secure DFT instruments are independently reachable in the same scan flow, each gated by exactly its own disable field and restored when it clears, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-35-dtp_ijtag_dfd_test]]
===== 3.5 `dtp_ijtag_dfd_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, OCAH-local iJTAG SIB model

*Objective*: DFD access

*Test Coverage*:

* DFD instrument reachable through the iJTAG network when its SIB is open (SELECT_IJTAG, IR=0x1A)
* `jtag_dfd_host_scan_ctrl_o` asserts shift control and data shifts correctly through `jtag_dfd_host_scan_out_o` / `jtag_dfd_host_scan_in_i`
* `dbg_disable_i.dfd = 1` forces the DFD SIB closed (gated), restored when the disable clears
* Eight seeded iterations request the DFD SIB open beside seeded DFT SIB requests under a seeded `dbg_disable_i.dfd`

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Load SELECT_IJTAG (IR=0x1A) via `OcahJtagMasterDriver`; open only the DFD SIB; Update-DR.
. Verify `jtag_dfd_host_scan_ctrl_o` asserts; shift a known pattern out on `jtag_dfd_host_scan_out_o` and read it back through `jtag_dfd_host_scan_in_i` via the OCAH-local SIB model.
. Assert `dbg_disable_i.dfd = 1`; attempt to open the DFD SIB.
a. Verify the SIB stays closed and none of the DFD scan controls pulse across the whole gated window.
. For eight seeded iterations, request the DFD SIB open with seeded requests for the two DFT SIBs under a seeded `dbg_disable_i.dfd`, and verify the chain length, the scan-control window, and the captures against the SIB model.
. Prove release without replay: with every disable clear, close all three SIBs; assert `dbg_disable_i.dfd` and attempt to open the DFD SIB under a temporal window in which none of its scan controls pulse; clear the disable without reset and verify, in a windowed close-everything scan, that no SIB select pulses. Then re-open the DFD SIB and verify DFD access is restored.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`dfd_access_chk` |`CHK-SCAN-WIN` and `CHK-SCAN-CHAIN`: DFD scan data shifts correctly when the SIB is open and ungated
|`dfd_gate_chk` |`CHK-SCAN-WIN` and `CHK-SCAN-CHAIN`: DFD SIB forced closed and its scan controls never pulse while `dbg_disable_i.dfd` is asserted
|`dfd_stored_chk` |`CHK-SCAN-WIN`: a gated Update-DR does not modify stored SIB state (no delayed open after release)
|`dfd_restore_chk` |`CHK-SCAN-WIN` and `CHK-SCAN-LEN`: DFD access resumes, without TAP reset, after the disable clears
|===

*Pass Criteria*: DFD is reachable when ungated and forced closed when `dbg_disable_i.dfd` is asserted, recovering without reset when the disable clears, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-36-dtp_3dcr_stap_sel_ds_test]]
===== 3.6 `dtp_3dcr_stap_sel_ds_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip` (primary TAP master and the downstream slave TAPs behind every STAP host port), OCAH-local STAP chain model

*Objective*: Downstream (I/O) STAP selection proved end to end against a downstream TAP

*Test Coverage*:

* TAP_3DCR (IR=0x0E) selects the I/O STAP by writing 3DCR `stap_sel=1`, `tms_hold=1`, `config_hold=1` through composed full-chain scans (the `TAP_3DCR` data register is the 2-bit PTAP 3DCR followed serially by the STAP SIB chain); a selected I/O STAP additionally splices one extra flop into the chain through its TDI lockup latch; every scan issued while a STAP is selected is composed over the full network, IR scans included, because the PTAP routes its instruction shift-out into the STAP chain
* A behavioral downstream TAP (`ocah_jtag_vip` slave device: 5-bit IR, distinct IDCODE, one writable `DS_TDR` of a distinct width) is spliced behind every STAP host port; its IR and selected data register become chain segments the reference model predicts independently of the device engine
* While selected, the downstream IDCODE reads back through the STAP, a network-wide IR scan selects `DS_TDR`, and a per-pass random value written through the chain reads back and is confirmed by the device's Update-DR record; the I/O host port forwards (`tdo_oen` pulses, `tms` follows the live TMS) as corroboration
* `dbg_disable_i.stap_io = 1` stops forwarding, parks the host `tms` at the stored `tms_hold=1` so the downstream TAP sits in Test-Logic-Reset, blocks a 3DCR update attempted while gated, and leaves the downstream register unchanged with no Update-DR recorded
* Once the disable clears, without any reset, the stored selection resumes: the downstream answers IDCODE again (park reset proven) and reloading `DS_TDR` returns the pre-gate value; an unrelated STAP's downstream TAP completes a full load/write/read cycle while the target stays gated and records no update; a fresh configuration recovers fully

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Flush the TAP_3DCR configuration chain (TRST clears it, and it holds through the over-length all-zero scan that follows), then configure the I/O STAP with composed scans: set the PTAP 3DCR `select=1, config_hold=1` in a scan of its own, open its SIB, then write its 3DCR `stap_sel=1, tms_hold=1, config_hold=1`.
. Run a state-maintaining chain scan inside a temporal window; verify the host `tdo_oen` pulses, host `tms` follows the live TMS, and the captured chain matches the reference model.
. Read the downstream IDCODE through the chain (`CHK-DS-IDCODE`); select `DS_TDR` with a composed IR scan; write a per-pass random value; verify the device recorded exactly one Update-DR with that value (`CHK-SLAVE-DR-UPDATE`, `CHK-SLAVE-DR-UPDATE-COUNT`); read it back through the chain (`CHK-DS-TDR-READBACK`).
. Assert `dbg_disable_i.stap_io = 1` and attempt a randomized deselecting 3DCR write:
a. Verify `tdo_oen` never pulses, `tms` parks at the stored `tms_hold=1`, and the host `trst_n` stays deasserted at every edge of the gated window, with no assertion from the disable through the end of the gated scan.
b. Verify the chain readback shows the update was ignored (the STAP captures its masked `stap_sel` as 0 while the stored payload survives).
c. Verify the downstream device recorded no Update-DR, still holds the written value (`CHK-DS-TDR-HOLD`), and has moved from Run-Test/Idle, its state when the disable is asserted, to Test-Logic-Reset (`CHK-DS-PARKED-TLR`).
. Clear the disable without reset; verify forwarding resumes from the stored selection, the downstream answers IDCODE again, and reloading `DS_TDR` reads back the pre-gate value (`CHK-DS-TDR-RESUME`).
. Re-assert the disable and configure a neighbor STAP; verify the neighbor forwards while the gated port stays quiet, the neighbor's downstream TAP completes a load/write/read cycle, and the gated port's device records no update (isolation).
. Clear all disables, flush (TRST, which resets the downstream TAPs to IDCODE, then a TAP_3DCR load and an over-length all-zero scan), and configure fresh; verify full recovery with a new `DS_TDR` write and readback.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`stap_ds_select_chk` |Selected I/O STAP forwards: `tdo_oen` pulses and `tms` follows the live TMS (`CHK-SCAN-WIN`)
|`stap_ds_readback_chk` |Composed chain readback matches the reference model in every state (`CHK-SCAN-CHAIN`)
|`stap_ds_downstream_chk` |Downstream IDCODE and `DS_TDR` read back through the selected STAP; the device's Update-DR record matches the write (`CHK-DS-IDCODE`, `CHK-DS-TDR-READBACK`, `CHK-SLAVE-DR-UPDATE`, `CHK-SLAVE-DR-UPDATE-COUNT`)
|`stap_ds_gate_chk` |With `dbg_disable_i.stap_io` asserted, forwarding stops, a gated 3DCR update is ignored, and the downstream TAP moves from Run-Test/Idle into Test-Logic-Reset through the parked TMS with its register frozen (`CHK-DS-TDR-HOLD`, `CHK-DS-PARKED-TLR`)
|`stap_ds_isolation_chk` |An unrelated STAP and its downstream TAP stay usable while the I/O STAP is gated
|`stap_ds_recover_chk` |Selection resumes without reset after release with the pre-gate downstream value intact (`CHK-DS-TDR-RESUME`), and a fresh configuration fully recovers
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The I/O STAP forwards only while selected and enabled, the downstream TAP behind it is programmable and readable exactly then, `dbg_disable_i.stap_io` alone gates it with downstream state preserved and no delayed effect, and recovery needs no reset, with every required `CHK-*` ID recorded and zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-37-dtp_3dcr_stap_sel_smc_test]]
===== 3.7 `dtp_3dcr_stap_sel_smc_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip` (primary TAP master and the downstream slave TAPs behind every STAP host port), OCAH-local STAP chain model

*Objective*: SMC STAP selection proved end to end against a downstream TAP

*Test Coverage*:

* TAP_3DCR (IR=0x0E) selects the SMC debug STAP by writing 3DCR `stap_sel=1`, `tms_hold=1`, `config_hold=1` through composed full-chain scans (the `TAP_3DCR` data register is the 2-bit PTAP 3DCR followed serially by the STAP SIB chain); every scan issued while a STAP is selected is composed over the full network, IR scans included, because the PTAP routes its instruction shift-out into the STAP chain
* A behavioral downstream TAP (`ocah_jtag_vip` slave device: 5-bit IR, distinct IDCODE, one writable `DS_TDR` of a distinct width) is spliced behind every STAP host port; its IR and selected data register become chain segments the reference model predicts independently of the device engine
* While selected, the downstream IDCODE reads back through the STAP, a network-wide IR scan selects `DS_TDR`, and a per-pass random value written through the chain reads back and is confirmed by the device's Update-DR record; the SMC host port forwards (`tdo_oen` pulses, `tms` follows the live TMS) as corroboration
* `dbg_disable_i.stap_smc = 1` stops forwarding, parks the host `tms` at the stored `tms_hold=1` so the downstream TAP sits in Test-Logic-Reset, blocks a 3DCR update attempted while gated, and leaves the downstream register unchanged with no Update-DR recorded
* Once the disable clears, without any reset, the stored selection resumes: the downstream answers IDCODE again (park reset proven) and reloading `DS_TDR` returns the pre-gate value; an unrelated STAP's downstream TAP completes a full load/write/read cycle while the target stays gated and records no update; a fresh configuration recovers fully

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Flush the TAP_3DCR configuration chain (TRST clears it, and it holds through the over-length all-zero scan that follows), then configure the SMC debug STAP with composed scans: set the PTAP 3DCR `select=1, config_hold=1` in a scan of its own, open its SIB, then write its 3DCR `stap_sel=1, tms_hold=1, config_hold=1`.
. Run a state-maintaining chain scan inside a temporal window; verify the host `tdo_oen` pulses, host `tms` follows the live TMS, and the captured chain matches the reference model.
. Read the downstream IDCODE through the chain (`CHK-DS-IDCODE`); select `DS_TDR` with a composed IR scan; write a per-pass random value; verify the device recorded exactly one Update-DR with that value (`CHK-SLAVE-DR-UPDATE`, `CHK-SLAVE-DR-UPDATE-COUNT`); read it back through the chain (`CHK-DS-TDR-READBACK`).
. Assert `dbg_disable_i.stap_smc = 1` and attempt a randomized deselecting 3DCR write:
a. Verify `tdo_oen` never pulses, `tms` parks at the stored `tms_hold=1`, and the host `trst_n` stays deasserted at every edge of the gated window, with no assertion from the disable through the end of the gated scan.
b. Verify the chain readback shows the update was ignored (the STAP captures its masked `stap_sel` as 0 while the stored payload survives).
c. Verify the downstream device recorded no Update-DR, still holds the written value (`CHK-DS-TDR-HOLD`), and has moved from Run-Test/Idle, its state when the disable is asserted, to Test-Logic-Reset (`CHK-DS-PARKED-TLR`).
. Clear the disable without reset; verify forwarding resumes from the stored selection, the downstream answers IDCODE again, and reloading `DS_TDR` reads back the pre-gate value (`CHK-DS-TDR-RESUME`).
. Re-assert the disable and configure a neighbor STAP; verify the neighbor forwards while the gated port stays quiet, the neighbor's downstream TAP completes a load/write/read cycle, and the gated port's device records no update (isolation).
. Clear all disables, flush (TRST, which resets the downstream TAPs to IDCODE, then a TAP_3DCR load and an over-length all-zero scan), and configure fresh; verify full recovery with a new `DS_TDR` write and readback.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`stap_smc_select_chk` |Selected SMC debug STAP forwards: `tdo_oen` pulses and `tms` follows the live TMS (`CHK-SCAN-WIN`)
|`stap_smc_readback_chk` |Composed chain readback matches the reference model in every state (`CHK-SCAN-CHAIN`)
|`stap_smc_downstream_chk` |Downstream IDCODE and `DS_TDR` read back through the selected STAP; the device's Update-DR record matches the write (`CHK-DS-IDCODE`, `CHK-DS-TDR-READBACK`, `CHK-SLAVE-DR-UPDATE`, `CHK-SLAVE-DR-UPDATE-COUNT`)
|`stap_smc_gate_chk` |With `dbg_disable_i.stap_smc` asserted, forwarding stops, a gated 3DCR update is ignored, and the downstream TAP moves from Run-Test/Idle into Test-Logic-Reset through the parked TMS with its register frozen (`CHK-DS-TDR-HOLD`, `CHK-DS-PARKED-TLR`)
|`stap_smc_isolation_chk` |An unrelated STAP and its downstream TAP stay usable while the SMC debug STAP is gated
|`stap_smc_recover_chk` |Selection resumes without reset after release with the pre-gate downstream value intact (`CHK-DS-TDR-RESUME`), and a fresh configuration fully recovers
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The SMC debug STAP forwards only while selected and enabled, the downstream TAP behind it is programmable and readable exactly then, `dbg_disable_i.stap_smc` alone gates it with downstream state preserved and no delayed effect, and recovery needs no reset, with every required `CHK-*` ID recorded and zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-38-dtp_3dcr_stap_sel_extra_test]]
===== 3.8 `dtp_3dcr_stap_sel_extra_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip` (primary TAP master and the downstream slave TAPs behind every STAP host port), OCAH-local STAP chain model

*Objective*: Extra STAP selection proved end to end against a downstream TAP

*Test Coverage*:

* TAP_3DCR (IR=0x0E) selects the extra STAP by writing 3DCR `stap_sel=1`, `tms_hold=1`, `config_hold=1` through composed full-chain scans (the `TAP_3DCR` data register is the 2-bit PTAP 3DCR followed serially by the STAP SIB chain); every scan issued while a STAP is selected is composed over the full network, IR scans included, because the PTAP routes its instruction shift-out into the STAP chain
* A behavioral downstream TAP (`ocah_jtag_vip` slave device: 5-bit IR, distinct IDCODE, one writable `DS_TDR` of a distinct width) is spliced behind every STAP host port; its IR and selected data register become chain segments the reference model predicts independently of the device engine
* While selected, the downstream IDCODE reads back through the STAP, a network-wide IR scan selects `DS_TDR`, and a per-pass random value written through the chain reads back and is confirmed by the device's Update-DR record; the `jtag_stap_extra_host_tap_ctrl_o[0]` port forwards (`tdo_oen` pulses, `tms` follows the live TMS) as corroboration
* `dbg_disable_i.stap_extra = 1` stops forwarding, parks the host `tms` at the stored `tms_hold=1` so the downstream TAP sits in Test-Logic-Reset, blocks a 3DCR update attempted while gated, and leaves the downstream register unchanged with no Update-DR recorded
* Once the disable clears, without any reset, the stored selection resumes: the downstream answers IDCODE again (park reset proven) and reloading `DS_TDR` returns the pre-gate value; an unrelated STAP's downstream TAP completes a full load/write/read cycle while the target stays gated and records no update; a fresh configuration recovers fully

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Flush the TAP_3DCR configuration chain (TRST clears it, and it holds through the over-length all-zero scan that follows), then configure the extra STAP with composed scans: set the PTAP 3DCR `select=1, config_hold=1` in a scan of its own, open its SIB, then write its 3DCR `stap_sel=1, tms_hold=1, config_hold=1`.
. Run a state-maintaining chain scan inside a temporal window; verify the host `tdo_oen` pulses, host `tms` follows the live TMS, and the captured chain matches the reference model.
. Read the downstream IDCODE through the chain (`CHK-DS-IDCODE`); select `DS_TDR` with a composed IR scan; write a per-pass random value; verify the device recorded exactly one Update-DR with that value (`CHK-SLAVE-DR-UPDATE`, `CHK-SLAVE-DR-UPDATE-COUNT`); read it back through the chain (`CHK-DS-TDR-READBACK`).
. Assert `dbg_disable_i.stap_extra = 1` and attempt a randomized deselecting 3DCR write:
a. Verify `tdo_oen` never pulses, `tms` parks at the stored `tms_hold=1`, and the host `trst_n` stays deasserted at every edge of the gated window, with no assertion from the disable through the end of the gated scan.
b. Verify the chain readback shows the update was ignored (the STAP captures its masked `stap_sel` as 0 while the stored payload survives).
c. Verify the downstream device recorded no Update-DR, still holds the written value (`CHK-DS-TDR-HOLD`), and has moved from Run-Test/Idle, its state when the disable is asserted, to Test-Logic-Reset (`CHK-DS-PARKED-TLR`).
. Clear the disable without reset; verify forwarding resumes from the stored selection, the downstream answers IDCODE again, and reloading `DS_TDR` reads back the pre-gate value (`CHK-DS-TDR-RESUME`).
. Re-assert the disable and configure a neighbor STAP; verify the neighbor forwards while the gated port stays quiet, the neighbor's downstream TAP completes a load/write/read cycle, and the gated port's device records no update (isolation).
. Clear all disables, flush (TRST, which resets the downstream TAPs to IDCODE, then a TAP_3DCR load and an over-length all-zero scan), and configure fresh; verify full recovery with a new `DS_TDR` write and readback.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`stap_extra_select_chk` |Selected extra STAP forwards: `tdo_oen` pulses and `tms` follows the live TMS (`CHK-SCAN-WIN`)
|`stap_extra_readback_chk` |Composed chain readback matches the reference model in every state (`CHK-SCAN-CHAIN`)
|`stap_extra_downstream_chk` |Downstream IDCODE and `DS_TDR` read back through the selected STAP; the device's Update-DR record matches the write (`CHK-DS-IDCODE`, `CHK-DS-TDR-READBACK`, `CHK-SLAVE-DR-UPDATE`, `CHK-SLAVE-DR-UPDATE-COUNT`)
|`stap_extra_gate_chk` |With `dbg_disable_i.stap_extra` asserted, forwarding stops, a gated 3DCR update is ignored, and the downstream TAP moves from Run-Test/Idle into Test-Logic-Reset through the parked TMS with its register frozen (`CHK-DS-TDR-HOLD`, `CHK-DS-PARKED-TLR`)
|`stap_extra_isolation_chk` |An unrelated STAP and its downstream TAP stay usable while the extra STAP is gated
|`stap_extra_recover_chk` |Selection resumes without reset after release with the pre-gate downstream value intact (`CHK-DS-TDR-RESUME`), and a fresh configuration fully recovers
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The extra STAP forwards only while selected and enabled, the downstream TAP behind it is programmable and readable exactly then, `dbg_disable_i.stap_extra` alone gates it with downstream state preserved and no delayed effect, and recovery needs no reset, with every required `CHK-*` ID recorded and zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip` (primary TAP master and the downstream slave TAPs behind every STAP host port), OCAH-local STAP chain model

*Objective*: SEP STAP selection proved end to end against a downstream TAP

*Test Coverage*:

[arabic]
. . Read the downstream IDCODE through the chain (`CHK-DS-IDCODE`); select `DS_TDR` with a composed IR scan; write a per-pass random value; verify the device recorded exactly one Update-DR with that value (`CHK-SLAVE-DR-UPDATE`, `CHK-SLAVE-DR-UPDATE-COUNT`); read it back through the chain (`CHK-DS-TDR-READBACK`).
. Assert `dbg_disable_i.stap_sep = 1` and attempt a randomized deselecting 3DCR write:
a. Verify `tdo_oen` never pulses, `tms` parks at the stored `tms_hold=1`, and the host `trst_n` stays deasserted at every edge of the gated window, with no assertion from the disable through the end of the gated scan.
b. Verify the chain readback shows the update was ignored (the STAP captures its masked `stap_sel` as 0 while the stored payload survives).

. Clear the disable without reset; verify forwarding resumes from the stored selection, the downstream answers IDCODE again, and reloading `DS_TDR` reads back the pre-gate value (`CHK-DS-TDR-RESUME`).
. Re-assert the disable and configure a neighbor STAP; verify the neighbor forwards while the gated port stays quiet, the neighbor's downstream TAP completes a load/write/read cycle, and the gated port's device records no update (isolation).
. Clear all disables, flush (TRST, which resets the downstream TAPs to IDCODE, then a TAP_3DCR load and an over-length all-zero scan), and configure fresh; verify full recovery with a new `DS_TDR` write and readback.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`stap_sep_select_chk` |Selected SEP debug STAP forwards: `tdo_oen` pulses and `tms` follows the live TMS (`CHK-SCAN-WIN`)
|`stap_sep_readback_chk` |Composed chain readback matches the reference model in every state (`CHK-SCAN-CHAIN`)
|`stap_sep_downstream_chk` |Downstream IDCODE and `DS_TDR` read back through the selected STAP; the device's Update-DR record matches the write (`CHK-DS-IDCODE`, `CHK-DS-TDR-READBACK`, `CHK-SLAVE-DR-UPDATE`, `CHK-SLAVE-DR-UPDATE-COUNT`)
|`stap_sep_gate_chk` |With `dbg_disable_i.stap_sep` asserted, forwarding stops, a gated 3DCR update is ignored, and the downstream TAP moves from Run-Test/Idle into Test-Logic-Reset through the parked TMS with its register frozen (`CHK-DS-TDR-HOLD`, `CHK-DS-PARKED-TLR`)
|`stap_sep_isolation_chk` |An unrelated STAP and its downstream TAP stay usable while the SEP debug STAP is gated

[[sec-310-dtp_ext_stap_scan_test]]
===== 3.10 `dtp_ext_stap_scan_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, OCAH-local STAP model, the 7-bit host segment in `tb_top`

*Objective*: Extended STAP scan

*Test Coverage*:

* Extended STAP scan interface (`+jtag_stap_host_scan_*+`) enabled via PTAP_3DCR.SELECT=1
* For this scenario `tb_top` places a 7-bit host segment, a scan register that captures its own update register, between `jtag_stap_host_scan_out_o` and `jtag_stap_host_scan_in_i`; the STAP chain model predicts it at the TDO end of the TAP_3DCR chain, so the captured bits and the position of a marker shifted ahead of the chain image show which return path the PTAP used
* `jtag_stap_host_scan_ctrl_o` pulses all four scan controls during DR operations, and a seeded value written into the segment through a composed TAP_3DCR scan reads back through `jtag_stap_host_scan_in_i`
* Clearing PTAP_3DCR.SELECT=0 removes the STAP chain from the scan path: all four host scan controls stay quiet across a DR scan, and TDO carries the PTAP 3DCR
* `dbg_disable_i.stap_host = 1` forces all four `jtag_stap_host_scan_ctrl_o` signals quiet across the whole gated window (temporal-window evidence) and bypasses scan-in: the PTAP chain return is `jtag_stap_host_scan_out_o`, so the marker arrives right behind the internal chain and the segment keeps its value
* Normal scan operation resumes, without any reset, after the disable clears: the segment is back in the return path with its pre-gate value

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Flush the TAP_3DCR configuration chain, write PTAP_3DCR.SELECT=1 in a scan of its own, then a seeded non-zero segment value in a composed scan via `OcahJtagMasterDriver`.
. Inside a temporal window, run a maintain scan with a seeded marker ahead of the chain image; verify all four host scan controls pulse, the captured chain (segment included) matches the reference model, and the marker arrives right behind the chain.
. Clear PTAP_3DCR.SELECT=0 with a composed scan and run a maintain scan under a temporal window: none of the four host scan controls pulses, TDO carries the PTAP 3DCR, and the marker arrives two bits later.
. Security gating:
a. Re-enable scan (PTAP_3DCR.SELECT=1 with a seeded CONFIG_HOLD) with a composed scan, then assert `dbg_disable_i.stap_host = 1`.
b. Inside a temporal window covering the whole gated maintain scan, verify none of the four host scan-control signals pulses and scan-in is bypassed: the captured chain and the marker position match the internal chain alone.
c. Clear the disable without reset and verify the four host scan controls pulse again, the segment reads back its pre-gate value, and the marker arrives behind the full chain.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`ext_stap_enable_chk` |The four host scan controls pulse during DR operations when PTAP_3DCR.SELECT=1 (`CHK-SCAN-WIN`), and the segment value and the marker return through `jtag_stap_host_scan_in_i` (`CHK-SCAN-CHAIN`)
|`ext_stap_deselect_chk` |With PTAP_3DCR.SELECT=0 all four host scan controls stay quiet across a DR scan (`CHK-SCAN-WIN`) and TDO carries the PTAP 3DCR with the marker two bits behind it (`CHK-SCAN-OBS`)
|`ext_stap_gate_chk` |With `dbg_disable_i.stap_host` asserted, all four host scan controls stay quiet across the gated window (`CHK-SCAN-WIN`) and scan-in is bypassed: the chain readback and the marker position match the internal chain alone (`CHK-SCAN-CHAIN`)
|`ext_stap_restore_chk` |Normal STAP host scan resumes, without reset, after the disable clears: the four host scan controls pulse and the segment reads back its pre-gate value (`CHK-SCAN-WIN`, `CHK-SCAN-CHAIN`)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The extended STAP scan controls pulse when selected, stay quiet when deselected, are suppressed while `dbg_disable_i.stap_host` is asserted with the PTAP chain return taken from `jtag_stap_host_scan_out_o`, and recover without reset with the segment value intact when it clears, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-311-dtp_3dcr_config_hold_test]]
===== 3.11 `dtp_3dcr_config_hold_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, OCAH-local STAP model

*Objective*: 3DCR config hold

*Test Coverage*:

* CONFIG_HOLD in both PTAP_3DCR and STAP 3DCR protects register contents across Test-Logic-Reset (TLR)
* With CONFIG_HOLD=1, SELECT (PTAP) and SELECT/TMS_HOLD (STAP) survive a forced TLR; TRST clears them at either CONFIG_HOLD value
* With CONFIG_HOLD=0, a TLR clears the affected 3DCR fields to 0
* Configuration values are exercised through composed TAP_3DCR chain scans (`OcahJtagMasterDriver`) against the STAP reference model, for every STAP in a seeded order, with a seeded STAP SELECT so the retained selection is proved spliced and unspliced
* `cg_3dcr_hold` `x_hold`: the PTAP 3DCR and every STAP 3DCR through Test-Logic-Reset and TRST at both CONFIG_HOLD values

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Flush the chain; write PTAP_3DCR with CONFIG_HOLD=h, SELECT=1 in a scan of its own, then open the STAP's SIB; Update-DR.
. Write the STAP 3DCR with CONFIG_HOLD=h, a seeded SELECT, TMS_HOLD=1; Update-DR.
. Force TLR (5x TMS=1), or TRST for the TRST sub-case; the IR returns to IDCODE.
. Reload TAP_3DCR with a composed IR scan (while the PTAP SELECT is set, every IR scan shifts through the STAP chain).
a. With the PTAP SELECT retained (h=1 across TLR), a maintain scan returns the chain: verify the captured PTAP SELECT=1 and CONFIG_HOLD=1.
b. With the PTAP SELECT cleared (h=0, or TRST), TDO carries the PTAP 3DCR itself: verify both bits read 0 and that a marker shifted behind them arrives two bits later (the chain return would delay it by the chain length).
. Re-select the PTAP and reopen the STAP's SIB; a maintain scan then carries the STAP 3DCR.
a. h=1 across TLR: verify SELECT and TMS_HOLD read back as written.
b. h=0 across TLR, or TRST: verify every STAP 3DCR field reads 0.
. Repeat the four sub-cases (h=1+TLR, h=0+TLR, h=1+TRST, h=0+TRST) in a seeded order for every STAP.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`config_hold_preserve_chk` |`CHK-SCAN-CHAIN`: with CONFIG_HOLD=1 the maintain scans after TLR capture PTAP SELECT=1/CONFIG_HOLD=1 and the STAP's written SELECT/TMS_HOLD
|`config_hold_clear_chk` |`CHK-SCAN-OBS` and `CHK-SCAN-CHAIN`: with CONFIG_HOLD=0 the PTAP 3DCR reads 0 over the TDR return path after TLR (marker position proves the path) and the reopened STAP 3DCR captures 0; TRST clears both regardless of CONFIG_HOLD
|`config_hold_data_chk` |`CHK-SCAN-CHAIN`: every chain readback (PTAP 3DCR, SIB, STAP 3DCR fields) matches the STAP reference model's predicted capture after each reset
|===

*Pass Criteria*: CONFIG_HOLD=1 holds both 3DCRs across TLR while CONFIG_HOLD=0 allows TLR to clear them, matching the reference model, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-312-dtp_3dcr_tms_hold_test]]
===== 3.12 `dtp_3dcr_tms_hold_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, OCAH-local STAP model

*Objective*: 3DCR TMS hold

*Test Coverage*:

* TMS_HOLD field in the STAP 3DCR sets the parked TMS value driven to a deselected STAP
* TMS_HOLD=1 parks the deselected STAP TMS high (drives toward TLR); TMS_HOLD=0 parks it low (drives toward RTI)
* Parked value is observed on the STAP TAP control output (`jtag_stap_<x>_tms`) for a whole maintain scan while the STAP is deselected (SELECT=0); the deselected port never drives `tdo_oen`
* Both TMS_HOLD polarities are exercised for every STAP, in a seeded order, through composed TAP_3DCR chain scans (`OcahJtagMasterDriver`)
* Every 3DCR payload (SELECT, TMS_HOLD, CONFIG_HOLD) on all four STAPs at once, enabled and under the four STAP disables: an enabled port forwards while its SELECT is set and otherwise parks at its TMS_HOLD, a disabled port parks at its stored TMS_HOLD whatever its SELECT, and every chain readback matches the reference model, closing `cg_stap_3dcr` `x_config`

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Flush the chain, set the PTAP SELECT in a scan of its own, then open the STAP's SIB; Update-DR.
. Select the STAP via its 3DCR (SELECT=1, TMS_HOLD=h); Update-DR.
. Under a temporal window over a maintain scan while the STAP is selected, count `jtag_stap_<x>_tms` and `jtag_stap_<x>_tdo_oen` high samples per TCK cycle: `tdo_oen` pulses, and the host TMS toggles and equals the primary TMS at every rising TCK edge. This is the positive control for the parked checks that follow, on the same port and the same window shape.
. Deselect the STAP (SELECT=0, TMS_HOLD=h); Update-DR.
. Under a temporal window over a maintain scan, count `jtag_stap_<x>_tms` and `jtag_stap_<x>_tdo_oen` high samples per TCK cycle.
a. h=1: TMS is high on every cycle (parked high toward TLR).
b. h=0: TMS is never high (parked low toward RTI).
c. `tdo_oen` never pulses.
. Compare the maintain scan's captured chain (SIB, SELECT=0, TMS_HOLD=h) against the STAP reference model.
. Repeat for h in {1, 0} in a seeded order and for every STAP in a seeded order.
. Flush the chain, set the PTAP SELECT in a scan of its own, and open all four SIBs; then for eight payload rounds in a seeded order, round r writing payload (r + i) mod 8 to the i-th STAP in one composed scan:
a. Under a temporal window over a maintain scan, verify each port forwards when its SELECT is set and otherwise parks at its TMS_HOLD with `tdo_oen` quiet, and the captured chain matches the reference model.
b. Assert the four STAP disables; under a temporal window over a maintain scan, verify every port parks at its stored TMS_HOLD with `tdo_oen` quiet, and the captured chain matches the reference model; clear the disables.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`tms_hold_selected_chk` |`CHK-SCAN-WIN`: while the STAP is selected its `tdo_oen` pulses and its TMS follows the live TMS across the maintain scan (positive control)
|`tms_hold_high_chk` |`CHK-SCAN-WIN`: with TMS_HOLD=1 the deselected STAP's TMS count equals the window's TCK count and `tdo_oen` stays quiet
|`tms_hold_low_chk` |`CHK-SCAN-WIN`: with TMS_HOLD=0 the deselected STAP's TMS count is 0 and `tdo_oen` stays quiet
|`tms_hold_data_chk` |`CHK-SCAN-CHAIN`: the maintain scan's captured 3DCR (SELECT=0, TMS_HOLD=h) matches the STAP reference model, which also supplies the expected parked polarity
|`tms_hold_sweep_chk` |`CHK-SCAN-WIN` and `CHK-SCAN-CHAIN`: in every payload round each port forwards or parks as its stored SELECT, TMS_HOLD, and disable call for, and both chain readbacks match the reference model
|===

*Pass Criteria*: A selected STAP forwards, and once deselected it parks its TMS at the configured TMS_HOLD value for both polarities, and every 3DCR payload forwards or parks as its fields and the STAP disables call for, matching the reference model, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-313-dtp_scan_dbg_disable_matrix_test]]
===== 3.13 `dtp_scan_dbg_disable_matrix_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, OCAH-local iJTAG SIB and STAP chain models

*Objective*: Debug-disable matrix over the eight scan-side gate fields

*Test Coverage*:

* Deterministic one-hot rows for `stap_io`, `stap_smc`, `stap_sep`, `stap_extra`, `stap_host`, `dft_secure`, `dft_nonsecure`, and `dfd`, plus the all-clear and all-disabled boundary masks and seeded multi-hot masks
* Per row: each iJTAG SIB requested open follows its disable (temporal window: a gated SIB's four scan controls never pulse, an effective SIB's select is seen high); each STAP configured through composed TAP_3DCR scans follows its disable (a gated port never forwards and its gated 3DCR write is ignored, verified by chain readback); the extended host scan interface follows `stap_host`
* After the all-disabled row: a gated open of all three SIBs, attempted under all eight gate fields after a sanctioned close, does not stick once every disable releases without reset (release without replay), and a full all-clear row recovers
* Mixed rows prove unrelated-resource isolation; in cocotb every checker-verified outcome samples its `DtpDbgDisableFcov` ledger cell, and the test requires all sixteen scan-side cells and emits the JSON coverage artifact; in both realizations `dtp_scan_fcov` records the same cells, the mask class, isolation, release, and recovery at the signal level in `cg_dbg_disable_scan`

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. For each row mask (all-clear, one-hot per field, seeded multi-hot, all-disabled last): drive the full disable vector and settle four toggling TCK cycles.
. iJTAG evidence: request all three SIBs open inside a temporal window and check each SIB's allowed/blocked outcome against the reference model.
. STAP evidence: with every disable clear, flush the TAP_3DCR chain, set the PTAP SELECT in a scan of its own, open all four SIBs and write all four 3DCR payloads in composed scans, then assert the row mask and run a windowed maintain scan; check per-port forwarding vs quiet with `tms` parked at the stored `tms_hold=1`, the host scan-control window, and the chain readback; then attempt a clearing 3DCR write under the mask and verify by chain readback that the gated ports ignore it and the ungated ports accept it.
. After the final all-disabled row, prove release without replay: with every disable clear, close all three SIBs in a sanctioned scan; assert all eight gate fields and attempt to open all three SIBs under a temporal window in which none of their scan controls pulse; release every disable without reset and verify, in a windowed close-everything scan, that no SIB select pulses (no delayed open). Then rerun the all-clear row as recovery.
. In cocotb, emit the `DtpDbgDisableFcov` JSON artifact and require all sixteen scan-side cells.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`scan_matrix_ijtag_chk` |`CHK-SCAN-WIN`, `CHK-SCAN-LEN`, and `CHK-SCAN-CHAIN`: each SIB's select/window outcome follows exactly its disable field
|`scan_matrix_stap_chk` |`CHK-SCAN-WIN` and `CHK-SCAN-CHAIN`: each STAP's forwarding outcome follows exactly its disable field; gated 3DCR writes are ignored
|`scan_matrix_host_chk` |`CHK-SCAN-WIN`: the extended host scan controls follow `stap_host`
|`scan_matrix_readback_chk` |`CHK-SCAN-CHAIN`: composed chain readbacks match the reference model under every mask
|`scan_matrix_noreplay_chk` |`CHK-SCAN-WIN`: no gated open attempt sticks after the disables release
|`scan_matrix_recovery_chk` |`CHK-SCAN-WIN`, `CHK-SCAN-LEN`, and `CHK-SCAN-CHAIN`: the all-clear row fully recovers after the all-disabled row
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every row mask produces the per-field allowed/blocked outcomes with temporal-window evidence, no delayed effects, and full recovery, closing all sixteen scan-side cells of `DtpDbgDisableFcov` and `cg_dbg_disable_scan`, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-314-dtp_stap_chain_hold_test]]
===== 3.14 `dtp_stap_chain_hold_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, OCAH-local STAP model

*Objective*: STAP chain holds while the PTAP 3DCR SELECT is clear

*Test Coverage*:

* With PTAP_3DCR.SELECT=0 the STAP chain's select, capture, shift and update strobes stay low through IR and DR scans under IDCODE and BYPASS, so no STAP SIB or 3DCR changes
* All-ones data, which would open every SIB and select every STAP were the chain clocked, is shifted under IDCODE and BYPASS in DR scans of 3, 4, 8 and a seeded 9 to 32 bits
* Once SELECT is set in a TAP_3DCR scan of its own, a composed chain readback shows every SIB closed

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Flush the TAP_3DCR configuration chain (TRST clears it).
. Under one temporal window over the host scan controls and every STAP's `tdo_oen` and `tms`: load IDCODE and shift all-ones DR scans of each width; load BYPASS with an all-ones IR scan and shift the same DR scans.
. Load TAP_3DCR, set PTAP_3DCR SELECT=1 in a scan of its own, then run a maintain scan and compare the captured chain against the STAP reference model.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`stap_chain_hold_quiet_chk` |`CHK-SCAN-WIN`: no host scan control pulses, no STAP `tdo_oen` pulses, and every STAP `tms` stays at its reset TMS_HOLD of 0 across the unselected scans
|`stap_chain_hold_readback_chk` |`CHK-SCAN-CHAIN`: the maintain scan captures every SIB closed, matching the reference model
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Scans with the PTAP 3DCR SELECT clear leave the STAP chain untouched, and the chain reads back closed once SELECT is set, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-4-xtrig-ctp-and-ctm-scenarios]]
==== 4. XTRIG, CTP, and CTM Scenarios

NOTE: Every scenario in this section ends each pass with `spill_contract_all`
(`CHK-XTRIG-AXIL`): since the last system reset, every CSR port spill register
(<<sec-48-dtp_xtrig_axi_channel_skew_test>>) has kept its input READY high exactly while it
holds fewer than two beats and its output VALID high exactly while it holds a beat
(`xtrig_axil_spill_err_count` reads 0).

[[sec-41-dtp_xtrig_wire_or_test]]
===== 4.1 `dtp_xtrig_wire_or_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_axi_vip`, OCAH-local CTP/CTM BFMs

*Objective*: CTP wire-OR

*Test Coverage*:

* CTP wire-OR mode (CONFIG.MODE=0) pulse synchronization with configurable pulse stretching
* Output pulse width equals (STRETCH_MULT{plus}1) clock cycles for a wide multiplier, the single-cycle minimum, and a seeded width in between
* A chiplet's pull of the CTP's shared CT_Req_out wire, at either INVERT sense, synchronized into the `clk_i` domain and delivered to the CTM destination: the port's `ct_dst` rises three clocks after the wire is pulled (two synchronizer stages and the registered output) and never when the wire is released
* STATUS.BUSY reads 1 over the CSR path while a pulse of at least nine cycles (STRETCH_MULT >= 8, the CSR read latency bound) is active and 0 once it ends; the registered BUSY tracks the output enable cycle for cycle at every stretch
* Internal cross-trigger pulse delivered to the CTM destination on an external trigger

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Configure a seeded CTP via `OcahAxiLiteMasterAgent`: CONFIG.MODE=0 (Wire-OR), CONFIG.INVERT=0, STRETCH_MULT=0x000F (16 clocks).
. Drive a single-cycle internal cross-trigger request to the CTP (via CTM routing).
